# Graph Report - vega  (2026-11-18)

## Corpus Check
- 3278 files · ~5,463,710 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 742 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 319, .scm 156, .wit 77)

## Summary
- 111792 nodes · 349230 edges · 2256 communities (1711 shown, 545 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 13397 edges (avg confidence: 0.76)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9c75592d`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
